Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The cycles variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 610 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 24 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 610 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the cycles structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 24. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
